# UI strings translated by the addon (ADR-014). One key per line: a Blizzard global-string name, or
# ItemSubClass:<classID>:<subClassID>. English comes from wago.tools at WAGO_BUILD (`make import-english`).
# Curation rules: never a key whose English is a name of a person, place, mob, item, spell or zone; colour codes
# (|c, |cn), line breaks, |4 plural grammar, textures (|T) and atlases (|A) are markup the Japanese keeps
# (ADR-016, pipeline/wfj/core/markup.py); a link (|H) only when it never changes (tests/python/test_ui_keys.py
# static_links), and never |K or $ tokens; keys that share one English must be drafted with one Japanese
# (`wfj validate` fails otherwise) unless one of them owns its Japanese (Core/UIStrings.lua OWN). Adding a
# key: list it here, `make import-english`, draft it, `make check`.

# as a debuff type. EmoteText keeps its %s slots: the chat surface fills them (a template fingerprint). ──
